# Senate Committee on Finance: TESDA - September 15, 2026

Source: https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=JA-qaBc74OA

AI-assisted working draft. AI can make errors. Always check quotations, names, rulings, and other consequential details against the official video.

**[00:09:45.300] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-000022)  
Budget briefing on the fiscal year 2027, proposed budget by the my most beloved agency, Technical Education and Skills Development Authority, or TESDA.

**[00:10:00.000] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-000024)  
Now, hereby called to order, we would like to acknowledge the entire TESDA family headed by our good friend, former Commissioner of ETCOM, Secretary Kiko Benitez.

**[00:10:16.920] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-000025)  
We would also acknowledge our DDGs. Hindi ko na ho kayo iisa-isahin. And of course, our friends from COA, the entire TESDA family. Magandang hapon po sa inyong lahat.

**[00:10:34.380] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-000026)  
So let me start by commending all of you for your service to our country and our people. Such a great honor and privilege.

**[00:10:46.920] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-000027)  
Na naging part po ako ng TESDA. At hanggang ngayon, I go with the TESDA man moniker. At muli ho, magandang hapon sa inyong lahat.

**[00:10:57.620] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-000028)  
TESDA has always held a special place in my heart bilang dating hepe ng TESDA. Personal po sa atin ang mission na bigyan ang bawat Pilipino ng kasanayan at pagkakataon na magkaroon ng marangal na trabaho at mas magandang buhay.

**[00:11:16.920] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-000029)  
Mas magandang kinabukasan. Sa likod po ng bawat TESDA scholarship na very famous, very popular, ay isang kabataan na naghahanap ng kanyang unang trabaho.

**[00:11:29.780] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-000030)  
Isa pong magulang na nagnanais madagdagan ang kita ng kanilang pamilya o isang manggagawang kailangan mag-upskill upang hindi po maiwan ng nagbabagong ekonomiya.

**[00:11:42.320] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-000031)  
In 2026, TESDA received its largest budget on record.

**[00:11:46.920] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-000032)  
But its current budget is only 2.2% of the total education budget. Laging sinasabi, ang TESDA ay partin ng tinatawag na trinity doon sa trifocalized education with the Department of Education and the Commission on Higher Education.

**[00:12:05.980] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-000033)  
At gaya ng trinity sa isang pananampalataya, yung TESDA daw yung tumatay yung Holy Spirit.

**[00:12:15.800] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-000034)  
Napakahalaga.

**[00:12:16.920] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-000035)  
Pero ang hirap intindihin.

**[00:12:18.300] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-000036)  
For 2027, TESDA is proposing a budget of 21.20 billion, 5.15 billion or 19.5% lower than its 2026 budget.

**[00:12:31.300] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-000037)  
Now, of this amount, 20.48 billion is intended for operations.

**[00:12:37.480] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-000038)  
Direct program allocations include 19.66 billion for the Technical Education Skills Development Program.

**[00:12:44.660] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-000039)  
497.6 million for the Regulatory Program and 40.1 million for the Policy Program.

**[00:12:53.780] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-000040)  
Sa bawat isang daang piso sa panukala ng budget ng TESDA, halos 93 pesos ang nakalaan sa Core Technical Education and Skills Development Program,

**[00:13:06.040] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-000041)  
which covers scholarships, training, and other skills development interventions.

**[00:13:10.860] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-000042)  
Now, with fewer resources and continuing demand for training,

**[00:13:15.560] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-000043)  
every peso must work harder and reach those who need it most.

**[00:13:20.340] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-000044)  
But a scholarship slot alone is not success.

**[00:13:23.720] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-000045)  
A TESDA scholarship must be a complete pathway from enrollment and quality training

**[00:13:29.720] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-000046)  
to assessment, certification, and ultimately decent work for a sustainable livelihood.

**[00:13:35.500] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-000047)  
Ngayong pong araw, nais po nating malaman.

**[00:13:38.300] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-000048)  
Nakakarating po ba ang mga scholarships sa mahihirap at underserved communities?

**[00:13:43.020] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-000049)  
Ang mga kursong pinupondohan ba natin ay talagang kailangan ng industriya? Nakapagtatapos nga po ba na-assess at nasa-certify ang ating mga scholar at ating mga kliyente? At nagkakaroon po ba sila ng trabaho angkop sa kanilang natututunan?

**[00:14:02.980] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-000050)  
We also want TESDA scholarships to prepare Filipinos for the future through higher qualifications, micro-credentials, digital and AI skills, enterprise-based training, and clear pathways for continuous learning and career advancement.

**[00:14:19.620] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-000051)  
TESDA is seeking additional funding for more than 162,000 beneficiaries. And of course, as always, we are here ready to support resources that will genuinely expand opportunity.

**[00:14:32.120] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-000052)  
But expansion must be matched also by implementation capacity, transparency, timely fund utilization, and measurable employment outcomes. Our purpose is very clear.

**[00:14:45.720] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-000053)  
We wanted to strengthen TESDA and its programs, but also ensure that every scholarship delivers on its promise to the Filipino people.

**[00:14:54.360] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-000054)  
Having said that, we look forward to a fruitful discussion on this proposed 2027

**[00:15:00.000] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-000055)  
budget of TESDA. Muli, marami pong

**[00:15:02.320] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-000056)

We have also accelerated

**[00:25:22.660] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000340)  
the development.

**[00:25:23.660] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000341)  
Thank you, again,

**[00:25:24.660] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000342)  
to the Houses of Congress

**[00:25:26.660] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000343)

**[00:25:54.660] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000355)  
for within the TVET ecosystem as well.

**[00:25:57.660] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000356)  
And we're doing this with Khan Academy.

**[00:26:02.660] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000357)  
Next slide.

**[00:26:04.660] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000358)  
And again,

**[00:26:06.660] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000359)  
because of the recommendations of EDCOM,

**[00:26:12.660] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000360)  
the initiative to increase

**[00:26:15.660] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000361)  
the higher level NCs has been ongoing,

**[00:26:18.660] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000362)  
including the review of the diploma program

**[00:26:21.660] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000363)  
currently to ensure

**[00:26:23.660] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000364)  
that they are level 5 PQF aligned.

**[00:26:28.660] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000365)  
We now have 1,412 diploma programs.

**[00:26:32.660] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000366)

**[00:26:35.660] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000367)  
in level 3 to level 4 courses since 2022.

**[00:26:39.660] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000368)  
And if you can see,

**[00:26:41.660] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000369)  
the percentage or ratio has been growing.

**[00:26:46.660] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000370)

**[00:26:48.660] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000371)  
of level 2s are still growing.

**[00:26:51.660] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000372)  
Because that's part of the industry demand.

**[00:26:54.660] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000373)  
But we have also focused attention

**[00:26:56.660] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000374)  
on ensuring alignment

**[00:26:58.660] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000375)

**[00:27:00.660] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000376)  
Of the 1,412 diploma programs registered,

**[00:27:06.660] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000377)  
648 have been reviewed for alignment

**[00:27:09.660] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000378)

**[00:27:12.660] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000379)  
that were released last January

**[00:27:15.660] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000380)

**[00:27:19.660] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000381)  
Next slide.

**[00:27:20.660] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000382)  
TESDA is scaling AI,

**[00:27:22.660] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000383)  
upscaling for BPO and digital jobs

**[00:27:26.660] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000384)  
as a fear that the country has

**[00:27:31.660] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000385)  
about AI disruption for jobs.

**[00:27:35.660] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000386)  
We're trying to make sure

**[00:27:37.660] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000387)  
that as many of our BPO's employees,

**[00:27:41.660] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000388)  
for example,

**[00:27:42.660] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000389)  
are AI-enabled

**[00:27:43.660] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000390)  
so that they can go with the times.

**[00:27:45.660] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000391)  
At the moment,

**[00:27:46.660] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000392)  
34,766 beneficiaries

**[00:27:49.660] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000393)  
and institution-based AI and data courses

**[00:27:52.660] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000394)  
have been identified.

**[00:27:54.660] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000395)  
And we have been involved

**[00:27:56.660] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000396)  
in crafting the AI upscaling roadmap,

**[00:27:59.660] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000397)  
which aims to achieve widespread,

**[00:28:01.660] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000398)  
or as widespread as possible,

**[00:28:03.660] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000399)  
AI literacy

**[00:28:05.660] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000400)  
to ensure that the country shifts

**[00:28:07.660] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000401)  
from being an AI consumer

**[00:28:09.660] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000402)  
to in fact being an AI builder.

**[00:28:12.660] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000403)  
10,000 BPO workers are being upscaled in AI

**[00:28:15.660] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000404)  
through enterprise-based training.

**[00:28:18.660] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000405)  
So that's 34,766 for institution-based,

**[00:28:22.660] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000406)  
an additional 10,000 for BPO workers

**[00:28:25.660] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000407)  
for enterprise-based training

**[00:28:27.660] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000408)  
for around 44,000.

**[00:28:29.660] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000409)  
This is in addition to any other

**[00:28:32.660] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000410)  
institution-based trainings

**[00:28:36.660] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000411)  
and micro-credentials

**[00:28:37.660] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000412)  
that we are rolling out.

**[00:28:39.660] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000413)  
TESDA has also been very much a part

**[00:28:42.660] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000414)  
of building the talent pipeline

**[00:28:44.660] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000415)  
for semiconductors and electronics industry,

**[00:28:46.660] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000416)  
which after all,

**[00:28:47.660] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000417)  
is one of the largest,

**[00:28:49.660] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000418)  
if not the largest,

**[00:28:51.660] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000419)  
manufactured goods exports of the country.

**[00:28:54.660] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000420)  
The Philippines Semiconductor Electronics Industry Roadmap

**[00:28:58.660] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000421)  
has just recently been launched.

**[00:29:00.660] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000422)  
And the roadmap targets

**[00:29:02.660] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000423)  
$70 billion in semiconductor revenues

**[00:29:06.660] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000424)  
and $40 billion in electronics revenues by 2030.

**[00:29:10.660] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000425)  
And that growth that's been dictated by government

**[00:29:15.660] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000426)  
will require skills

**[00:29:16.660] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000427)  
will require skilled workforce.

**[00:29:20.660] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000428)  
140,000 have already been assessed

**[00:29:23.660] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000429)  
on electronics assembly skills

**[00:29:25.660] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000430)  
since January 2025.

**[00:29:27.660] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000431)  
This is in compliance

**[00:29:31.660] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000432)  
with the advisory council

**[00:29:33.660] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000433)  
created under AO39,

**[00:29:35.660] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000434)  
which has requested that TESDA

**[00:29:38.660] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000435)  
provide skills training for 128,000 workers

**[00:29:42.660] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000436)  
for the sector actually by 2028,

**[00:29:45.660] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000437)  
I believe.

**[00:29:46.660] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000438)  
So we're slightly above schedule

**[00:29:48.660] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000439)  
or ahead of schedule

**[00:29:49.660] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000440)  
in terms of number of beneficiaries.

**[00:29:51.660] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000441)  
Our next piece is trying to make sure,

**[00:29:53.660] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000442)  
in fact,

**[00:29:54.660] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000443)  
that they are appropriately linked

**[00:29:56.660] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000444)  
to the right qualifications requested.

**[00:30:00.000] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000445)  
By industry and at the right levels. So along that line, we have also piloted a level 5 diploma program skills ladder with engineering degrees in semiconductors and electronics with CHED piloted in Cebu with, I believe, 18 higher education institutions in Region 7.

**[00:30:22.640] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000446)  
Next slide.

**[00:30:30.000] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000447)  
For the next slide, in conjunction with our special provisions in the 2026 GAHA, TESDA is upskilling daycare workers and partnering with LGUs for local skills needs.

**[00:30:43.640] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000448)  
So far, 9,989 CDWs were served under the ECCD program with ongoing training for at least 1,000 to 2,000 more.

**[00:30:55.180] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000449)  
And we aim to train an additional 9,231.

**[00:31:00.000] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000450)  
CDWs in 2027 in accordance, in fact, with the new ECCD law.

**[00:31:06.600] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000451)  
We have also piloted waste management training, for example, with LGUs.

**[00:31:12.300] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000452)  
And here's an example with Coca-Cola Foundation and the city of Naga.

**[00:31:20.020] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000453)  
Next slide.

**[00:31:22.620] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000454)  
So ito po yung isang tinanong ni Senator Joel kanina.

**[00:31:28.000] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000455)  
The employment rate of TVET graduates.

**[00:31:30.000] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000456)  
In 2025, was at 81.64% based on our survey methods.

**[00:31:38.000] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000457)  
We are trying to scale up E-BET because E-BET graduates have a employment rate of almost 90% based on our data.

**[00:31:49.740] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000458)  
And as of August 2026, 1,299 E-BET programs have been registered as part of our implementation.

**[00:31:59.100] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000459)  
It's also part of our implementation.

**[00:32:00.000] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000460)  
So we have the World Bank project, Senator Villanueva, to make sure that we can further granularize and expand and strengthen our surveying methods as well as use the skills passport to track and encode data sets of training completely, totally to trabaho as much as possible.

**[00:32:22.000] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000461)  
Next slide.

The entrance of E-BET, I mean E-BET clients.

**[00:32:46.620] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000467)

So next slide, please.

**[00:33:05.500] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000474)  
This one naman po is our flexible delivery modalities.

**[00:33:10.680] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000475)  
Favorite program din po ni Senator Villanueva, ang TESDA online program.

**[00:33:16.200] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000476)  
We would like to point out that 1.6 million learners have enrolled in 225 courses.

**[00:33:24.720] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000477)  
Courses on AI fundamentals and environmental literacy are now actually available.

**[00:33:30.000] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000478)  
Available on TUP as well as financial literacy, entrepreneurship, among others.

**[00:33:36.920] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000479)

**[00:33:55.380] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000480)  
There's a sample of potential courses available in TUP.

**[00:34:00.000] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000481)  
We have complete full NC qualifications online, as well as short-term courses that might be more life-skills oriented and not necessarily job or employment specific.

Assessments being done online?

**[00:34:15.960] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000483)

**[00:34:16.600] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000484)

**[00:34:20.280] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000485)  
Yes, sir.

**[00:34:24.240] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000486)  
The next slide is our ongoing SIPTIVETS project.

**[00:34:30.000] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000499)  
With ADB, in order to expand our TIVET capacity nationwide through regional innovation centers and potentially developing them into full-fledged polytechnics.

**[00:34:44.500] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000500)  
There are meant to be three batches.

**[00:34:47.800] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000501)  
Two batches are actually ongoing, covering eight Arctic projects now under construction.

**[00:34:53.580] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000502)  
Batch one is targeted to be completed by the fourth quarter of this year.

**[00:34:58.380] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000503)  
And batch two

**[00:34:59.560] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000504)  
In 2027.

**[00:35:01.260] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000505)  
Batch three is ongoing with a new site in Negros Island region.

**[00:35:07.320] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000506)  
They're all designed to be finished by the end of 2027 or the first quarter of 2028 at the latest.

**[00:35:19.240] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000507)  
Next slide.

**[00:35:21.320] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000508)  
With our convergence, Mr. Chair, with OWWA and DMW, TESDA has been bringing free assessments

**[00:35:29.560] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000509)  
to our OFWs as well as boosting skills of repatriated OFWs that come back.

**[00:35:37.040] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000510)  
The TESDA overseas assessment program brings skills assessment and national certification directly to Filipino workers abroad.

**[00:35:45.200] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000511)  
And we have current partners in Saudi Arabia, UAE, Singapore, Hong Kong, Malaysia, Qatar, Kuwait, and South Korea.

**[00:35:53.300] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000512)  
With more countries being developed upon the request, in fact, of OWWA and DMW themselves.

**[00:35:59.560] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000515)  
Italy is already tentatively scheduled. New Zealand and London are under exploration as the demand for overseas assessment for OFWs increase.

**[00:36:13.940] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000516)  
As part of our harmonization and again as part of the EDCOM recommendations, Mr. Chair, we have assessed 427,000 senior high school learners to boost job readiness of K-12 graduates.

**[00:36:33.600] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000517)  
We have 6,143 teachers trained to become assessors since 2024 with more intended.

**[00:36:43.940] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000518)  
For 2027, TESDA is proposing to train 4,888 teachers in their NC courses and in fact 23,845 teachers in training methodology to deliver in the TechPro programs in the strengthened senior high school, which will be rolled out for grade 12 this school year.

**[00:37:11.840] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000519)  
We are also

**[00:37:13.520] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000520)  
opening ladders as part of the seamless pathways recommended by One Education with CHED, on the other hand.

**[00:37:22.220] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000521)  
We have Philippine credit transfer systems, which are ladderized for dental hygiene, agriculture and biosystems engineering, midwifery, pharmacy, and ongoing for approval are hospitality and tourism management, radiologic technologies, nutrition and dietetics.

**[00:37:43.520] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000522)  
Among others.

**[00:37:45.100] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000523)  
So this is an ongoing program that we are rolling out in accordance with the ladderization law.

**[00:37:54.620] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000524)  
PCTS guidelines are under development for ECCD, medical technology, occupational therapy, and physical therapy programs.

**[00:38:02.260] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000525)  
Again, in compliance, Mr. Chair, with the recommendations of EDCOM as well.

**[00:38:09.440] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000526)  
We are trying to increase the number of HEIs

**[00:38:13.540] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000527)  
that will also roll out both TVI programs and/or recognize Philippine credit transfer systems.

**[00:38:22.840] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000528)  
So our 2027 proposed budget.

**[00:38:25.860] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000529)  
All that, Mr. Chair, is to point out how little funding we have in order to address the needs and the demands that we are held accountable for.

**[00:38:35.560] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000530)  
Okay.

**[00:38:41.420] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000531)  
So,

**[00:38:42.500] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000532)  
as I said,

**[00:38:43.520] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000533)  
Highlight of the history of our budget.

**[00:38:48.100] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000534)  
TESDA received its largest budget on record in 2026,

**[00:38:52.560] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000535)  
the same year that the Philippines devoted the highest share or percentage of its GDP to education in its history,

**[00:38:59.340] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000536)  
where 2.2% of the total education budget in 2026 was allocated to TESDA.

**[00:39:06.320] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000537)  
For NEP in 2027, Mr. Chair, bumaba po kami to 1.8.

**[00:39:10.800] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000538)  
1.88% na lang ng full education budget.

**[00:39:16.660] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000539)  
And as you pointed out, yes po, yung 2.2 naging 1.88.

**[00:39:22.180] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000540)  
At gaya po ng sinabi ninyo, almost 20% po ang binaba ng NEP 2027 sa GAA 2026.

**[00:39:31.680] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000541)  
Higher budget is key to sustaining TESDA's momentum,

**[00:39:34.900] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000542)  
as well as our ability to address the Lifelong Learning Framework Act, Mr. Chair,

**[00:39:39.440] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000543)  
for that entire, your entire workforce age population.

**[00:39:47.640] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000544)  
The 2027 NEP, in fact, is 46.8% lower than the proposed TESDA budget,

**[00:39:57.420] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000545)  
and 20% lower than GAA 2026.

**[00:40:04.620] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000546)  
Our TESDA utilization by September 2026 is actually,

**[00:40:09.440] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000547)  
higher compared to the same period last year.

**[00:40:12.780] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000548)  
We have recurring problems, Mr. Chair.

**[00:40:17.720] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000549)  
Our capital outlay from the SIPTIVET projects and the,

**[00:40:25.420] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000550)  
for late release, for example, of funds,

**[00:40:28.820] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000551)  
affects our ability to perform our funds utilization properly.

**[00:40:35.300] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000552)  
Without those, sir, we are at around 40,

**[00:40:39.800] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000553)  
almost 43 to 45, I think, percent by September.

**[00:40:50.640] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000554)  
So, here's our additional request na lang, sir.

**[00:40:54.000] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000555)  
Sana naman, we can get as close to and maybe a little bit more

**[00:40:58.820] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000556)  
than our 2026 GAAT project, budget, no,

**[00:41:02.120] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000557)  
to ensure at least that we sustain and maintain our delivery.

**[00:41:08.080] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000558)  
Ah.

**[00:41:08.760] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000559)  
There is a request for an additional 933,000,000 for TWSP to gain an additional 37,356 additional beneficiaries.

**[00:41:23.880] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000560)  
For STEP, sir, 777,000,000 for an additional 24,758 additional beneficiaries.

**[00:41:33.320] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000561)  
Mr. Chair, I would like to flag maybe the most important request of TESDA in terms of fund augmentation, which is 2.4 billion for UACTEA, for free TVETSER for higher education.

**[00:41:47.200] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000562)  
This is our diploma programs so that we can actually expand our new entrance for 46,430 new beneficiaries.

**[00:41:57.580] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000563)  
Mr. Chair, I would like to simply point out that this increase in UACTEA funding,

**[00:42:03.320] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000564)  
is partly because of our push for higher level qualifications.

**[00:42:08.200] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000565)  
We need to make sure that in compliance with both free TVETSER and the newly amended UNIFAST law, or UACTEA law,

**[00:42:18.000] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000566)  
that we keep pace of our support to our students as the demand, in fact, also increases.

**[00:42:25.740] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000567)  
In addition, to ensure the proper implementation of TESDA training programs

**[00:42:31.380] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000568)  
within the new emerging technologies,

**[00:42:33.320] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000569)  
we are also working on a new program,

**[00:42:35.880] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000570)  
which was called the TechPro of senior high school.

**[00:42:40.100] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000571)  
We would like to request 600 or so million for 28,733 TechPro teachers for senior high school,

**[00:42:49.380] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000572)  
and 622 million thereabouts,

**[00:42:52.980] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000573)  
of training primarily for barangay-based enterprises,

**[00:42:59.020] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000574)  
and or barangay,

**[00:43:01.080] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000575)  
community-based healthcare, and so on.

**[00:43:03.320] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000576)  
So this is sir, for BHW training, for barangay nutrition scholars, as part of the other side,

**[00:43:10.700] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000577)  
Mr. Chair of the ECCD-CDW training for anti-stunting that we would like to roll out and capacitate on the barangay level.

**[00:43:19.800] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000578)  
And lastly, perhaps we are incredibly glad that it is Senator Joel that is our sponsor for our bill

**[00:43:30.100] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000579)  
because we would like to flag that this is a very recent occurrence.

**[00:43:41.960] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000580)  
So we hosted for the first time last year, after 30 years, WorldSkills ASEAN in Manila.

**[00:43:51.360] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000581)

**[00:44:00.100] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000582)

**[00:44:02.940] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000583)

**[00:44:11.400] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000584)

**[00:44:18.300] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000585)

**[00:44:26.720] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000586)

**[00:44:30.100] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000587)

**[00:44:31.240] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000588)

**[00:44:37.380] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000589)

**[00:44:40.880] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-000590)

**[02:09:51.680] EDCOM 2 Executive Director Karol Mark Yee** (asr-002350)  
Mr. Chair, just to share in our studies,

**[02:09:54.600] EDCOM 2 Executive Director Karol Mark Yee** (asr-002351)  
before siguro mga 10 years ago,

**[02:09:56.940] EDCOM 2 Executive Director Karol Mark Yee** (asr-002352)  
there was just 20 recognized LUCs.

**[02:09:59.580] EDCOM 2 Executive Director Karol Mark Yee** (asr-002353)  
Now there are 113.

**[02:10:01.760] EDCOM 2 Executive Director Karol Mark Yee** (asr-002354)  
And more and more are applying to establish by law.

**[02:10:05.520] EDCOM 2 Executive Director Karol Mark Yee** (asr-002355)  
And the challenge po to that is when you look at their finances,

**[02:10:09.080] EDCOM 2 Executive Director Karol Mark Yee** (asr-002356)  
itatayo nila sa start.

**[02:10:11.680] EDCOM 2 Executive Director Karol Mark Yee** (asr-002357)  
Pero lahat ng susunod na gastos,

**[02:10:13.380] EDCOM 2 Executive Director Karol Mark Yee** (asr-002358)  
naka-charge against national government through free higher education.

**[02:10:17.000] EDCOM 2 Executive Director Karol Mark Yee** (asr-002359)  
So ang nangyayari, mas dependent pa sila sa FHE kaysa SUCs.

**[02:10:23.140] EDCOM 2 Executive Director Karol Mark Yee** (asr-002360)  
Kasi talagang totally dependent.

**[02:10:25.200] EDCOM 2 Executive Director Karol Mark Yee** (asr-002361)  
And bigla nang nagpo-pull out.

**[02:10:26.940] EDCOM 2 Executive Director Karol Mark Yee** (asr-002362)  
Nang funding mismo yung LGU when it comes to the sustaining of the LUCs.

**[02:10:32.780] EDCOM 2 Executive Director Karol Mark Yee** (asr-002363)  
And CHED has admitted in many of our technical working groups, sir,

**[02:10:36.820] EDCOM 2 Executive Director Karol Mark Yee** (asr-002364)  
in the committee, na talagang hindi na kaya nilang i-check yun.

**[02:10:40.280] EDCOM 2 Executive Director Karol Mark Yee** (asr-002365)  
Kasi ang hingin lang nila, five-year institutional plan,

**[02:10:43.220] EDCOM 2 Executive Director Karol Mark Yee** (asr-002366)  
certification that you have the resources to fund,

**[02:10:46.260] EDCOM 2 Executive Director Karol Mark Yee** (asr-002367)  
syempre they will certify.

**[02:10:47.680] EDCOM 2 Executive Director Karol Mark Yee** (asr-002368)  
But actually, do they fund eventually?

**[02:10:50.040] EDCOM 2 Executive Director Karol Mark Yee** (asr-002369)  
Hindi po talagang nangyayari.

**[02:10:51.420] EDCOM 2 Executive Director Karol Mark Yee** (asr-002370)  
Which is why the LUC governance bill that is in the committee now

**[02:10:54.640] EDCOM 2 Executive Director Karol Mark Yee** (asr-002371)  
will really play a

**[02:10:55.580] EDCOM 2 Executive Director Karol Mark Yee** (asr-002372)  
significant role

**[02:10:56.680] EDCOM 2 Executive Director Karol Mark Yee** (asr-002375)  
in clarifying the responsibilities

**[02:10:59.960] EDCOM 2 Executive Director Karol Mark Yee** (asr-002376)  
and making sure that hindi nagpo-pull out si LGU.

**[02:11:03.620] EDCOM 2 Executive Director Karol Mark Yee** (asr-002377)  
Dapat, counterpart, LGU is still funding,

**[02:11:07.180] EDCOM 2 Executive Director Karol Mark Yee** (asr-002378)  
but the national government will still support through FHE.

**[02:11:10.800] EDCOM 2 Executive Director Karol Mark Yee** (asr-002379)  
Pero hindi pwedeng pull out, tapos bahala na si NGA at si GAA

**[02:11:15.240] EDCOM 2 Executive Director Karol Mark Yee** (asr-002380)  
sa lahat ng funding ng LUCs.

**[02:11:17.060] EDCOM 2 Executive Director Karol Mark Yee** (asr-002381)  
Kasi talagang hindi po natin kakayanin yung lobo na

**[02:11:20.160] EDCOM 2 Executive Director Karol Mark Yee** (asr-002382)  
lahat, inasa lahat sa national government.

**[02:11:23.000] EDCOM 2 Executive Director Karol Mark Yee** (asr-002383)  
Tapos biglang, by the second year, wala nang pinupondo si LGU.

**[02:11:26.120] EDCOM 2 Executive Director Karol Mark Yee** (asr-002384)  
So that's really a challenge, sir, in quality and also in the sustainability.

**[02:11:31.200] EDCOM 2 Executive Director Karol Mark Yee** (asr-002385)  
Thank you.

**[02:28:59.080] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-002693)  
definitely AI will disrupt

**[02:29:02.560] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-002694)  
many of our industries,

**[02:29:04.520] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-002695)  
specifically the business process outsourcing.

**[02:29:08.280] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-002696)  
So I just want to get an update

**[02:29:10.280] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-002697)  
dito sa 1.8 billion

**[02:29:12.220] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-002698)  
sa TWSP

**[02:29:14.080] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-002699)  
and then ilan yung ating mga

**[02:29:16.340] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-002700)  
na-upskill

**[02:29:18.880] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-002701)  
na mga batas,

**[02:29:19.820] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-002702)  
na mga batas,

**[02:29:20.100] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-002703)  
on AI, semiconductors,

**[02:29:22.860] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-002704)  
and healthcare.

**[02:29:26.220] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-002705)  
Mr. Chair,

**[02:29:27.480] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-002706)  
if your permission,

**[02:29:28.700] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-002707)  
I would like another voice.

**[02:29:30.160] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-002708)  
Kanina pa kasi ako sumasagot.

**[02:29:32.260] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-002709)  
Si DDG Galo will respond.

**[02:29:34.540] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-002710)  
Si Galo, okay yan.

**[02:29:35.500] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-002711)  
Malakas kumain yan.

**[02:29:38.160] TESDA Deputy Director General Galo B. Glino III** (asr-002712)  
Thank you, Mr. President.

**[02:29:39.620] TESDA Deputy Director General Galo B. Glino III** (asr-002713)  
With the permission of our good secretary,

**[02:29:42.120] TESDA Deputy Director General Galo B. Glino III** (asr-002714)  
I will respond, Mr. President.

**[02:29:44.540] TESDA Deputy Director General Galo B. Glino III** (asr-002715)  
Mr. President, you are right.

**[02:29:46.640] TESDA Deputy Director General Galo B. Glino III** (asr-002716)  
We recognize po

**[02:29:47.940] TESDA Deputy Director General Galo B. Glino III** (asr-002717)  
the risk

**[02:29:49.100] TESDA Deputy Director General Galo B. Glino III** (asr-002718)  
risk

**[02:29:49.640] TESDA Deputy Director General Galo B. Glino III** (asr-002719)  
that is brought by AI

**[02:29:53.140] TESDA Deputy Director General Galo B. Glino III** (asr-002720)  
to our labor force.

**[02:29:56.280] TESDA Deputy Director General Galo B. Glino III** (asr-002721)  
Depending on who you ask,

**[02:29:59.180] TESDA Deputy Director General Galo B. Glino III** (asr-002722)  
the numbers vary,

**[02:30:02.360] TESDA Deputy Director General Galo B. Glino III** (asr-002731)  
But more or less around 30 percent, Mr. Chair, of our labor force and jobs are at risk, will be transformed or exposed to AI.

**[02:30:15.200] TESDA Deputy Director General Galo B. Glino III** (asr-002732)  
And we are very thankful, Mr. President, for the support of you and the Senate for the inclusion of a budget in the 2026 GAAA to support upskilling on AI.

**[02:30:30.180] TESDA Deputy Director General Galo B. Glino III** (asr-002733)  
Mr. President, we are happy to report that for 2026, for data and AI skills,

**[02:30:44.580] TESDA Deputy Director General Galo B. Glino III** (asr-002734)  
we have already allocated around 30,000 slots.

**[02:30:55.380] TESDA Deputy Director General Galo B. Glino III** (asr-002735)  
And for semiconductors, Mr. Chair, we have here already allocations for around 6,000 slots, Mr. President.

**[02:31:10.820] TESDA Deputy Director General Galo B. Glino III** (asr-002736)  
Ongoing pa po, Mr. President, ang pag-implementation.

**[02:31:14.580] TESDA Deputy Director General Galo B. Glino III** (asr-002737)  
At natin ang mga programa na ito, we are scaling up our AI upskilling through partners like STI,

**[02:31:23.660] TESDA Deputy Director General Galo B. Glino III** (asr-002738)  
nationwide networks of institutions that will deliver the courses, but also through Test the Online program, our e-learning platform,

**[02:31:33.540] TESDA Deputy Director General Galo B. Glino III** (asr-002739)  
where already we have around 90,000 enrollments in AI courses, Mr. President.

**[02:31:41.340] TESDA Deputy Director General Galo B. Glino III** (asr-002740)  
Tuloy-tuloy po ito, Mr. President.

**[02:31:43.420] TESDA Deputy Director General Galo B. Glino III** (asr-002741)  
Thank you, Mr. President.

**[02:31:44.580] TESDA Deputy Director General Galo B. Glino III** (asr-002742)  
And we hope to continue to be able to do this in 2027 with support of the Senate.

**[02:31:51.640] TESDA Deputy Director General Galo B. Glino III** (asr-002743)  
Thank you, po.

**[02:31:52.460] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-002744)  
DDG, itong you mentioned 30% of jobs at risk.

**[02:31:58.120] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-002745)  
So, based on statistics, mayroon tayong 49 million workforce.

**[02:32:07.100] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-002746)  
So, 30% is about 10 million, more or less.

**[02:32:10.740] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-002747)  
So, in other words, 10 million of our...

**[02:32:14.580] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-002748)  
10 million of the workforce is at risk of being affected by artificial intelligence.

**[02:32:20.420] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-002749)  
Yes, Mr. President.

**[02:32:20.900] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-002750)  
But, obviously, maliit pa lang yung ating na-upskill, no?

**[02:32:26.580] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-002751)  
Only 30,000 lang.

**[02:32:29.120] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-002752)  
So, in your analysis, of course, we need more funds to upgrade.

**[02:32:36.360] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-002753)  
But in your analysis, what should be the priority in terms of protecting jobs from AI?

**[02:32:44.580] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-002754)  
For example, BPO.

**[02:32:47.360] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-002755)  
Kasi BPO, mayroon tayong about 1.5, 1.6 million employees.

**[02:32:54.280] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-002756)  
And marami sa kanila mga entry-level jobs, no?

**[02:32:57.740] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-002757)  
Kasi puro voice calls.

**[02:33:00.100] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-002758)  
And I am predicting that AI will have a massive effect on them.

**[02:33:07.060] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-002759)  
Dahil next time, kausap na ng tao, artificial intelligence.

**[02:33:10.380] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-002760)  
So, for example, BPO, particular, no?

**[02:33:13.620] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-002761)  
How do we...

**[02:33:14.580] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-002762)  
How do we help the BPO industry survive the AI revolution

**[02:33:20.240] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-002763)  
so that AI will be part of the BPO industry and still grow our BPO industry?

**[02:33:29.940] TESDA Deputy Director General Galo B. Glino III** (asr-002764)  
Mr. President, we are following the AI upskilling roadmap

**[02:33:33.400] TESDA Deputy Director General Galo B. Glino III** (asr-002765)  
that we crafted together with the Commission on Higher Education

**[02:33:40.480] TESDA Deputy Director General Galo B. Glino III** (asr-002766)  
and the Analytics and AI Association of the Philippines.

**[02:33:44.580] TESDA Deputy Director General Galo B. Glino III** (asr-002767)  
So, kasama po sa roadmap na ito, ang efforts at plano natin to...

**[02:33:51.140] TESDA Deputy Director General Galo B. Glino III** (asr-002768)  
So, mayroon tayong roadmap?

**[02:33:52.160] TESDA Deputy Director General Galo B. Glino III** (asr-002769)  
Yes, sir.

**[02:33:52.460] TESDA Deputy Director General Galo B. Glino III** (asr-002770)  
In other words?

**[02:33:52.900] TESDA Deputy Director General Galo B. Glino III** (asr-002771)  
Yes, sir.

**[02:33:53.180] TESDA Deputy Director General Galo B. Glino III** (asr-002772)  
Okay.

**[02:33:53.940] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-002773)  
Can you describe to us ano ba yung mga skills, upskilling, or even reskilling ng AI skills?

**[02:34:02.580] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-002774)  
Can you...

**[02:34:03.540] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-002775)  
Kasi, to be honest about it, like kami dito, we just use it for research.

**[02:34:08.480] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-002776)  
Yes, sir.

**[02:34:08.780] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-002777)  
But what type of upskilling or even reskilling is TESTA doing to...

**[02:34:14.420] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-002778)  
Let's say, the BPO industry or the other industries?

**[02:34:17.680] TESDA Deputy Director General Galo B. Glino III** (asr-002779)  
Sir, the roadmap segmentizes our population and our labor market and labor force

**[02:34:23.700] TESDA Deputy Director General Galo B. Glino III** (asr-002780)  
into basically three segments that need, first, literacy,

**[02:34:30.880] TESDA Deputy Director General Galo B. Glino III** (asr-002781)  
which is foundational AI skills and awareness.

**[02:34:38.100] TESDA Deputy Director General Galo B. Glino III** (asr-002782)  
Next will be a segment for more specialized jobs,

**[02:34:44.420] TESDA Deputy Director General Galo B. Glino III** (asr-002783)  
and then, lastly, Mr. Chair, is really advanced courses

**[02:34:50.940] TESDA Deputy Director General Galo B. Glino III** (asr-002784)  
to equip a segment of our labor force to become builders of AI platforms and AI systems.

**[02:35:01.620] TESDA Deputy Director General Galo B. Glino III** (asr-002785)  
Not just consumers of AI, but as builders of AI.

**[02:35:06.440] TESDA Deputy Director General Galo B. Glino III** (asr-002786)  
At yan din po ang isinusulong natin sa TESTA, Mr. Chair.

**[02:35:11.260] TESDA Deputy Director General Galo B. Glino III** (asr-002787)  
While we started this year with...

**[02:35:13.540] TESDA Deputy Director General Galo B. Glino III** (asr-002788)  
Yes.

**[02:35:13.880] TESDA Deputy Director General Galo B. Glino III** (asr-002789)  
Around 30,000 slots for AI literacy and for foundational skills,

**[02:35:19.640] TESDA Deputy Director General Galo B. Glino III** (asr-002790)  
next year, we are hoping to move towards more specialized and advanced courses

**[02:35:24.320] TESDA Deputy Director General Galo B. Glino III** (asr-002791)  
to equip segments of our labor force to build AI systems.

**[02:35:32.240] TESDA Deputy Director General Galo B. Glino III** (asr-002792)  
Sa BPO sector po, this means that they will also be able to provide higher value services,

**[02:35:39.940] TESDA Deputy Director General Galo B. Glino III** (asr-002793)  
not just customer service,

**[02:35:42.720] TESDA Deputy Director General Galo B. Glino III** (asr-002794)  
but also to create...

**[02:35:43.880] TESDA Deputy Director General Galo B. Glino III** (asr-002795)  
to create products for clients abroad.

**[02:35:47.400] TESDA Deputy Director General Galo B. Glino III** (asr-002796)  
Ayan po ang kasama sa roadmap natin, Mr. Chair.

**[02:35:51.960] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-002797)  
For 2027, is this roadmap funded, adequately funded?

**[02:36:01.740] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-002798)  
Kasi nakita ko dito yung TWSP bumaba by 45%.

**[02:36:09.580] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-002799)  
From 3.6 billion, naging...

**[02:36:13.880] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-002800)  
I think it's 3.6 billion next year, no?

**[02:36:28.020] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-002801)  
In budget.

**[02:36:29.140] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-002802)  
So, bumaba siya by 45%.

**[02:36:31.440] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-002803)  
So, it was mas malaki siya last year.

**[02:36:34.680] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-002804)  
So, in other words, with that roadmap, maganda meron tayong roadmap,

**[02:36:42.000] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-002805)  
pero bumaba naman yung ating...

**[02:36:43.880] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-002806)  
scholarship for AI,

**[02:36:46.220] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-002807)  
how will this affect the upskilling and reskilling program of our workforce?

**[02:36:55.260] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-002808)  
Malit na nga dati, lumit pa ngayon,

**[02:36:59.080] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-002809)  
and there's a big chance na maapektuhan itong...

**[02:37:02.880] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-002810)  
I just want to make it...

**[02:37:04.880] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-002811)  
I just want to make the BPO industry as an example

**[02:37:09.860] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-002812)  
kasi siya yung ating saving grace for our economy.

**[02:37:13.880] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-002813)  
So, baka magkaroon tayo ng malaking problema

**[02:37:18.880] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-002814)  
kung hindi natin pupondohan itong AI upskilling and reskilling natin.

**[02:37:24.420] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-002815)  
We're looking for TESDA to really bridge that gap.

**[02:37:28.780] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-002816)  
Yes, sir.

**[02:37:29.300] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-002817)  
The gap meaning from our present low-value BPO industry

**[02:37:35.940] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-002818)  
to a high-value AI-driven BPO industry.

**[02:37:39.540] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-002819)  
And I see that TESDA is the agency that will bridge that.

**[02:37:43.880] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-002820)  
Mr. Chair, with your permission,

**[02:37:47.940] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-002821)  
actually, the funds that we use for the AI upskilling

**[02:37:52.980] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-002822)  
is not just in the TWSP.

**[02:37:55.740] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-002823)  
So, TWSP as an institutional base,

**[02:37:58.820] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-002824)  
this is part of the effort.

**[02:38:01.620] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-002825)  
This includes, of course, as Mas already mentioned,

**[02:38:04.880] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-002826)  
our partnerships with networks like STI and so on

**[02:38:08.780] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-002827)  
that might have the reach and the infrastructure

**[02:38:10.860] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-002828)  
to disseminate the training

**[02:38:12.900] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-002829)  
as far as the AI upskilling.

**[02:38:13.880] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-002830)  
As far as possible.

**[02:38:15.200] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-002831)  
We also have micro-credentials that are being rolled out,

**[02:38:19.300] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-002832)  
particularly for those that might be near-hireable,

**[02:38:22.540] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-002833)  
except they don't have the AI skills,

**[02:38:24.400] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-002834)  
but also for those that need upskilling within the BPO industry.

**[02:38:28.640] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-002835)  
And the third is we also have EBET funds from TTSP,

**[02:38:32.780] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-002836)  
Mr. Chair, to augment the funding sources.

**[02:38:37.080] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-002837)  
And we're hoping as many dual training

**[02:38:40.260] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-002838)  
and or EBET modalities with BPOs

**[02:38:42.940] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-002839)  
can be rolled out in that direction.

**[02:38:46.360] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-002840)  
But you're right,

**[02:38:47.680] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-002841)  
even if the three funding sources of TESDA

**[02:38:51.800] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-002842)  
are still lacking in the absolute scale

**[02:38:53.840] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-002843)  
or in the absolute numbers,

**[02:38:55.980] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-002844)  
even in the BPO industry.

**[02:38:59.360] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-002845)  
That's also why we were hoping that as much training

**[02:39:02.720] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-002846)  
that can be done by MOOCs, for example,

**[02:39:06.840] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-002847)  
so for free online training

**[02:39:08.720] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-002848)  
curated by the enterprises themselves,

**[02:39:12.940] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-002849)  
whether they avail of EBET subsidies

**[02:39:16.800] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-002850)  
or incentives from government,

**[02:39:19.600] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-002851)  
they do actually exist.

**[02:39:22.960] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-002852)  
So one is to offer it through the massive open online courses

**[02:39:27.880] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-002853)  
of TESDA itself

**[02:39:29.020] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-002854)  
or in partnership with other providers

**[02:39:32.700] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-002855)  
like Microsoft, Google, Amazon.

**[02:39:38.400] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-002856)  
They are all readily made available now

**[02:39:41.920] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-002857)  
on the TESDA online.

**[02:39:42.940] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-002858)  
The second is really a convergence

**[02:39:46.100] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-002859)  
with agencies like the ICT and so on and so forth.

**[02:39:49.300] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-002860)  
The other national government agencies

**[02:39:51.020] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-002861)  
also have some training funds.

**[02:39:54.140] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-002862)  
So ideally, what we can do is create a delivery unit

**[02:39:59.060] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-002863)  
that can monitor and evaluate the impact

**[02:40:02.100] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-002864)  
of all of these pockets.

**[02:40:03.860] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-002865)  
Kasi kahit na ito,

**[02:40:05.120] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-002866)  
samang mo po lahat-lahat yan,

**[02:40:06.880] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-002867)  
hindi ho tayo aabot sa 10 million Pilipinos.

**[02:40:09.500] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-002868)  
So it will be...

**[02:40:12.940] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-002869)  
small in comparison to the absolute needs

**[02:40:16.180] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-002870)  
and maybe a mechanism

**[02:40:18.260] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-002871)  
which I believe the PISAC is recommending to the president

**[02:40:22.400] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-002872)  
to combine and create a delivery unit

**[02:40:25.500] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-002873)  
that can monitor and evaluate

**[02:40:27.160] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-002874)  
the implementation of the AI roadmap

**[02:40:29.480] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-002875)  
might be appropriate.

**[02:40:31.180] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-002876)  
But to speak directly to your question,

**[02:40:34.740] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-002877)  
we have three potential sources of funds,

**[02:40:39.100] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-002878)  
all of which will be insufficient.

**[02:40:41.720] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-002879)  
We have our partnership

**[02:58:08.880] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-003399)  
RA

**[02:58:10.180] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-003400)  
12063.

**[02:58:14.300] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-003401)  
And alam naman natin,

si Secretary was

**[02:58:17.300] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-003403)

**[02:58:49.380] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-003416)  
This is just the number of registrations.

**[02:58:50.960] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-003418)  
This is just the number of registered programs

**[02:58:52.160] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-003419)

**[02:58:55.320] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-003420)

**[02:58:57.440] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-003421)

**[02:58:58.760] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-003422)

**[02:59:04.600] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-003423)  
for their certification and registration.

**[02:59:07.200] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-003424)  
So it's just that transition, sir.

**[02:59:09.200] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-003425)  
But moving forward,

**[02:59:10.280] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-003426)  
obviously, we would like that to grow

**[02:59:13.280] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-003427)

**[02:59:16.120] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-003428)  
And if you remember,

**[02:59:18.960] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-003429)  
nakalagay po sa,

**[02:59:19.960] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-003430)  
wala na si Rose,

**[02:59:20.960] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-003431)  
nag-ban yung ata,

**[02:59:22.240] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-003432)  
sa national TASD plan,

**[02:59:24.280] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-003433)

**[02:59:37.360] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-003437)

**[02:59:44.640] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-003439)  
But that's the intent.

**[02:59:45.900] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-003440)  
I really appreciate the questions raised by our Senate President.

**[02:59:56.620] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-003441)  
Kasi looking at the funding, no?

**[03:00:00.000] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-003442)  
Yung Tulong Trabaho Fund, which is, we started with 1 billion, now it's, if I'm not mistaken, 1.65 billion for 2027, as I saw it a while ago. Dito rin kinukuha yung EBET, diba? Am I right?

**[03:00:20.540] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-003443)  
Yes, sir. Because at the moment, most of the demand for EBET has been in upskilling. Dito po nang gagaling ang tinitraining namin for the BPO industry for upskilling. Because they are employees that are looking for upskilling, if you remember.

**[03:00:35.440] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-003444)  
Yes, yes, yes. I'm aware of that. It's just that for me, I think it should not be the only funding na pwede nating pagkuhanan.

**[03:00:46.440] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-003445)  
And in fact, I would suggest that we...

**[03:00:50.540] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-003446)  
We separate the SP from EBET. Para yung concerns ni SP, which I also agree. Sayang yung opportunity and we can focus on enterprise base in such a way that we'll be able to figure out how else we can work on it, improve.

**[03:01:12.700] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-003447)  
And looking at the historical background of the financial program.

**[03:01:20.540] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-003448)  
I think that in terms of TTSP, hindi naman nag-grow ng malaki eh. So kung doon lang yung universe mo eh, from...

**[03:01:27.640] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-003449)  
Hindi naman po.

**[03:01:29.100] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-003450)  
Getting from EBET, parang kulang.

**[03:01:31.220] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-003451)  
Ano po, nasa batas ho ninyo na pwede pong kumuha rin sa TWSP. So pwede pong TWSP at TTSP.

**[03:01:39.200] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-003452)  
Exactly. That's why ako ang suggestion ko, I mean, at least look into it. Baka magandang ihiwalay-lagyan natin ang sariling item, yung EBET. Diba?

**[03:01:50.020] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-003453)  
Pwede po.

**[03:01:50.540] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-003454)  
Pwede po. Para mas ano na.

**[03:01:52.280] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-003455)  
Pwede pwede po.

**[03:01:53.040] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-003456)  
Mas klaro. And they just gave me an idea when I was listening to our Senate President. Yeah, please, please, please continue.

**[03:02:01.580] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-003457)  
And Secretary, meron bang... Kasi merong... I think it's also the number of enterprises that join. So I remember talking to you about this na I think one of the parang concern or one of the complication,

**[03:02:20.540] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-003458)  
there's a... At least dun sa na-experience ko, kailangan pa i-amend ng corporation ng kanilang Articles of Incorporation.

**[03:02:31.880] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-003459)  
So, syempre sa kanila, parang hassle pa. Tapos, of course, kukuha ka pa ng abogado. So may mga ganun na maliliit na bagay. And also the claiming of incentives. I think this was also raised by EDCOM.

**[03:02:47.480] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-003460)  
So may mga ganitong...

**[03:02:50.540] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-003461)  
Overlook lang na agency na maliliit na bagay na sa atin maliit pero sa participating companies, hassle. Hassle sa kanila. Kaya huwag na lang. Huwag na tayo sumali dyan. So have you taken a look at this issue?

**[03:03:04.880] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-003462)  
Yes, sir. And in fact, it's not just the amendment of the Articles of Incorporation to include education and training as a provision. It's also things like TESDA guidelines, sir, required a...

**[03:03:19.880] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-003463)  
Ano talaga ba dyan? Notarized contract, sir, between the EBET provider or the enterprise and the learner. So we've tried to review all those guidelines in order to streamline them.

**[03:03:35.140] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-003464)  
We are hoping to comply with the EBET provisions on a digital one-stop shop by the fourth quarter, sir, of this year. So we're hoping that many of those little issues in terms of implementation...

**[03:03:49.880] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-003465)  
can be overcome before we launch the digital one-stop shop. I am assuming that even after we do so, may lalabas pa rin po yan na mahirap na kailangan ding palitan ng TESDA in terms of its internal rules and guidelines.

**[03:04:07.760] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-003466)  
But yes, sir, we understand that these are parts of the problem because the law, again, of Senator Joel...

**[03:04:19.880] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-003467)  
It's TVI's from registering. So the enterprise has to take the lead in terms of its registration. And many of those enterprises actually are reluctant to create training plans and so on, would rather partner up as that kind of dual training system with an institution.

**[03:04:40.380] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-003468)  
But the institution cannot, in fact, like they used to before the institution could register and partner up with an enterprise for the...

**[03:04:49.880] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-003469)  
enterprise-based portion. Ngayon po, baliktad. And the TVI institution, the TECVOC institution cannot be the one to register, even if they are on behalf of the enterprise, right?

**[03:05:07.420] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-003470)  
Kung kahit na-partner sila, which is another one of those hurdles. But it is, sir, it's in the law.

**[03:05:14.460] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-003471)  
Kung ngayon, yung enterprise, they need to partner.

**[03:05:18.200] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-003472)  
Not necessarily, sir.

**[03:05:19.880] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-003473)  
But in many ways, that's probably the...

**[03:05:23.860] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-003474)  
Most practical.

**[03:05:24.460] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-003475)  
Most practical for them to partner up with the TVI. But in fact, make the TVI help generate the training plan.

**[03:05:34.000] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-003476)  
And they can actually be part of the assessment program, di ba?

**[03:05:37.540] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-003477)  
Yes, sir.

**[03:05:38.320] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-003478)  
Yes, sir.

**[03:05:39.080] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-003479)  
Enterprises.

**[03:05:39.840] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-003480)  
Yes, sir.

**[03:05:41.320] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-003481)  
The last na lang siguro is one of the things that we've been looking forward to aside from the one-stop shop.

**[03:05:50.040] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-003482)

**[03:05:57.860] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-003483)  
Kasi wala po sa budget namin.

**[03:06:01.860] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-003484)  
EBET integrators po for small and micro enterprises that might not otherwise have the capacity to generate the training plans and so on needed, no?

**[03:06:13.660] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-003485)  
Or have the size and scale.

**[03:06:17.620] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-003486)  
Nasa batas nyo rin po yan.

**[03:06:18.800] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-003487)  
Yes, I was just...

**[03:06:19.720] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-003488)  
Just given the, you know, yung three points that still pending, no?

**[03:06:24.720] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-003489)  
Itong eBET support integrators, itong eBET one-stop shop, and itong eBET grievance mechanism still pending.

**[03:06:33.720] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-003490)

**[03:06:40.660] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-003491)  
There's no guidelines pa, e.

**[03:06:42.700] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-003492)  
Di ba?

**[03:06:43.560] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-003493)  
Wala pa yung guidelines on mechanism on addressing disputes.

**[03:06:48.000] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-003494)  
Disputes on eBET implementations?

**[03:06:50.960] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-003495)  
Yes, sir.

**[03:06:51.660] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-003496)  
So, we will come up with that also like in the next two weeks.

**[03:06:55.860] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-003497)  
Tama ba?

**[03:06:56.860] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-003498)  
It's going through the process.

**[03:06:59.580] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-003499)  
Sige.

**[03:07:00.160] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-003500)  
Sorry, SP.

**[03:07:01.260] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-003501)  
Please continue.

**[03:07:02.780] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-003502)  
Bottom line lang, Secretary, is just take a look at the...

**[03:07:06.460] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-003503)  
Those mga maliliit na bagay lang, no?

**[03:07:09.000] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-003504)

**[03:07:12.900] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-003505)

**[03:07:17.840] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-003506)  
Especially for eBET.

**[03:07:19.420] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-003507)

**[03:07:24.620] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-003508)

**[03:07:26.540] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-003509)

**[03:08:17.020] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-003518)  
Thank you.

**[03:08:17.380] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-003519)  
Thank you.

**[03:08:17.820] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-003520)

**[03:08:22.180] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-003521)  
Just a few more questions, no?

**[03:08:24.440] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-003522)  
Let me go to another topic that is very dear to me, itong lifelong learning.

**[03:08:32.000] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-003523)  
Given that TESDA has a central role in TVET and skills development under this particular law

**[03:08:40.100] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-003524)  
that we all supported, by the way, and as part of EDCOM, we are all espousing itong Lifelong Learning

**[03:08:47.800] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-003526)  
Development Framework Act.

**[03:08:49.860] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-003527)

**[03:09:00.700] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-003528)  
Kasi ano yan eh, as of August 31, diba, part of the law, yung section 14, provides that initial implementation

**[03:09:11.920] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-003529)  
shall be funded from the existing budgets of the concerned agencies, no?

**[03:09:15.620] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-003530)

**[03:09:17.800] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-003531)

**[03:09:23.760] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-003532)  
2026, anyway, merong na-obligate dito, no?

**[03:09:28.260] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-003533)  
In the disperse? Wala.

**[03:09:30.280] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-003534)  
For lifelong learning.

**[03:09:32.260] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-003535)  
Ang ginawa namin, sa totoo lang, as you know, kalalabas lang po ng IRR

**[03:09:39.380] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-003536)  
at hindi pa po lumalabas ang master plan ng PQF National Coordinating Council.

**[03:09:45.860] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-003537)  
And in the absence of that, in terms of targeting lifelong learning efforts per se,

**[03:09:51.400] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-003538)  
what we have been doing has been using our current budgets for things like creative industries, right?

**[03:10:04.760] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-003539)  
For things that may not look like they are directly related to employment per se,

**[03:10:13.080] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-003540)  
but might be for culture, for heritage.

**[03:10:15.860] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-003541)  
So on and so forth.

**[03:10:18.180] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-003542)  
All of that, sir, is going to be part of the lifelong learning rollout, particularly for LGUs,

**[03:10:23.240] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-003543)  
which is also, after all, part of the proposal to get DILG to spearhead its implementation.

**[03:10:31.280] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-003544)  
So sa ngayon, sir, yun pa lang ang aming mga nilalagay or ginagawa.

**[03:10:35.820] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-003545)  
Wala pa pong identified line item that says this is lifelong learning.

**[03:10:40.880] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-003546)  
I was about to say that.

**[03:10:45.860] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-003547)  
I'm happy to note that the Secretary of TESDA is now the chairperson of the PQF National Referencing Committee.

**[03:10:55.260] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-003548)

Yeah, but considering that you are there, nasa kanila yung bola, at least maano natin yan.

**[03:11:05.680] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-003550)  
And of course, that brings me to my next point, how much allocated for 2026 and for 2027.

**[03:11:15.860] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-003551)  
Kasi laki nung responsibility na binibigay sa TESDA's work under the Philippine Qualifications Framework

**[03:11:25.740] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-003552)  
and the National Referencing Committee.

At the moment?

We can get a line item for that?

**[03:11:53.780] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-003561)

**[03:11:58.220] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-003562)

**[03:11:59.900] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-003563)

**[03:12:04.960] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-003565)

**[03:13:11.280] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-003580)  
This year.

**[03:13:11.820] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-003581)  
Okay.

**[03:13:12.080] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-003582)

**[03:13:15.220] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-003583)

**[03:13:16.280] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-003584)

**[03:13:18.540] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-003585)  
Thank you.

**[03:13:19.240] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-003586)  
Sorry, Mr. Chair, if I may line just to be even more specific.

**[03:13:23.060] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-003587)

**[03:13:38.080] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-003588)

**[03:13:42.080] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-003589)

**[03:13:43.080] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-003590)

**[03:13:54.680] TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco "Kiko" Benitez** (asr-003591)  
And the timeline is supposed to be by December this year.

**[03:13:59.720] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-003592)  
Thank you.

**[03:14:00.680] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-003593)  
Thank you very much.

**[03:14:01.780] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-003594)  
Unless our Senate President or anyone would like to, perhaps, I don't want to preempt the Senate President's motion.

**[03:14:12.080] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-003595)  
To deem the, I mean, to submit for plenary consideration the…

Mr. Chairman, it's a pleasure to submit the budget of TESDA to plenary for further discussions and approval.

**[03:14:28.140] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-003597)

**[03:14:38.400] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-003599)

**[03:14:42.080] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-003601)
